				script.type = "text/javascript";
				// If the browser is Internet Explorer
				if (script.readyState){
					script.onreadystatechange = function() {
						if (script.readyState == "loaded" || script.readyState == "complete") {

					};

<script type="text/javascript">

    jQuery( function() {
        var requiredCheckboxes = jQuery(':checkbox[required]');

        requiredCheckboxes.click(function(){
           console.log(jQuery(this).parent().parent().parent('.ndn-field-checkbox').find(':checkbox').is(':checked'));
            if(jQuery(this).parent().parent().parent('.ndn-field-checkbox').find(':checkbox').is(':checked')) {
                jQuery(this).parent().parent().parent('.ndn-field-checkbox').find(':checkbox[required]').removeAttr('required');
            }

            else {
                jQuery(this).parent().parent().parent('.ndn-field-checkbox').find(':checkbox').attr('required', 'required');
            }
      });//
      window.verifyRecaptchaCallback = function (response) {

          jQuery('input[data-recaptcha]').val(response).trigger('change');

      }

      window.expiredRecaptchaCallback = function () {

          jQuery('input[data-recaptcha]').val("").trigger('change');

      }

    })
</script>
